36
37 //======================================================================================================================
38 //! @defgroup horner kyosu::horner
39 //! @ingroup agnostic
40 //! @brief Implement the horner scheme to evaluate polynomials
41 //! @{
42 //! @var horner
43 //!
44 //! If \f$(a_i)_{0\le i\le n-1}\f$ denotes the coefficients of the polynomial by decreasing
45 //! power order, the Horner scheme evaluates the polynom \f$p\f$ at \f$x\f$ by :
46 //! \f$\displaystyle p(x) = (((a_0x+a_1)x+ ... )x + a_{n-1})\f$.\n
47 //! For non commutative cases it is a left-horner scheme: coefficients are at the left of the x powers.
48 //!
49 //! using the `right` semantic modifier allows to use a right-horner scheme: coefficients are at the right of the x powers).
50 //!
51 //! @groupheader{Header file}
52 //!
53 //! @code
54 //! #include <kyosu/functions.hpp>
55 //! @endcode
56 //!
57 //! @groupheader{Callable Signatures}
58 //!
59 //! @code
60 //! namespace kyosu
61 //! {
62 //! template< auto T, auto C ...> auto horner(T x, C ... coefs) noexcept; //1
63 //! template< auto C, auto K> auto horner(T x, K tup) noexcept; //2
64 //!
65 //! // Semantic Modifiers
66 //! template<auto T, auto C ...> auto horner[left](T x, C ... coefs) noexcept; //1
67 //! template<auto C, auto K> auto horner[left]r(T x, K tup) noexcept; //2
68 //! template<auto T, auto C ...> auto horner[right](T x, C ... coefs) noexcept; //3
69 //! template<auto C, auto K> auto horner[right]r(T x, K tup) noexcept; //3
70 //!
71 //! }
72 //! @endcode
73 //!
74 //!! **Parameters**
75 //!
76 //! * `x` : real or cayley-dickson argument.
77 //! * `coefs...` : real or cayley-dickson arguments. The coefficients by decreasing power order
78 //! * `tup` : kumi tuple containing The coefficients by decreasing power order.
79 //!
80 //! **Return value**
81 //!
82 //! 1. Polynom is evaluated at x the other inputs are the polynomial coefficients.
83 //! The value of the polynom at `x` is returned, according to the formula:
84 //! \f$\displaystyle p(x) = (((a_0x+a_1)x+ ... )x + a_{n-1})\f$.\n
85 //! For non commutative cases it is a left-horner scheme.
86 //! 2. Polynom is evaluated at x the other input is a kumi tuple containing the coefficients
87 //! 3. the `right` modifyier is useful only when dealing wirh cayley-dickson non commutative algebras.\n
88 //! The value of the polynom at `x` is returned, according to the formula:
89 //! \f$\displaystyle p(x) = (x (x (x a_0+a_1)+ ... )+a_{n-1})\f$.\n
90 //! Of course for real or complex entries `left` and `right` leads to the same result
91 //!
92 //! **Notes**
93 //!
94 //! If the coefficients are simd values of cardinal N, this means you simultaneously
95 //! compute the values of N polynomials.
96 //! * If x is scalar, the polynomials are all computed at the same point
97 //! * If x is simd, the nth polynomial is computed on the nth value of x
98 //!
99 //! @groupheader{External references}
100 //! * [Wikipedia: Horner's method](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Horner%27s_method)
101 //!
102 //! @groupheader{Example}
103 //! @godbolt{doc/horner.cpp}
104 //======================================================================================================================
105 inline constexpr auto horner = eve::functor<horner_t>;
106 //======================================================================================================================
107 //! @}
108 //======================================================================================================================
109 }
110
111 namespace kyosu::_
112 {
113 template<typename X, concepts::cayley_dickson_like Z, typename... Zs, eve::callable_options O>
114 64708 KYOSU_FORCEINLINE constexpr auto horner_(KYOSU_DELAY(), O const& o, X xx, Z z, Zs... zs) noexcept
115 {
116 if constexpr ((concepts::real<X> && ... && concepts::real<Zs>))
117 {
118 43921 return eve::horner[o](xx, z, zs...);
119 }
120 else
121 {
122 using r_t = as_cayley_dickson_like_t<X, Z, Zs...>;
123 20787 constexpr size_t N = sizeof...(Zs);
124
125 if constexpr (N == 0) return r_t(0);
126 else if constexpr (N == 1) return convert(z, eve::as_element<r_t>{});
127 else
128 {
